Bowl at y=0.231 and plate at 0.2175 → bowl is +y = image right of the plate center by 1.4cm. So the bowl hangs slightly beyond the plate center in +y. Since plate radius ~0.055 and bowl radius ~0.055, a 1.4cm offset means the bowl footprint extends 1.4cm beyond the plate edge... no: bowl center at plate center +1.4cm means the bowl still covers the plate mostly, but the "On" predicate typically requires the bowl's centroid over the plate region: the bowl's center must lie within the plate. 1.4cm offset is fine.

Actually, let me be more careful about the plate's true center.
